# Aster Wallet Production Refactor Tracker

This is the release-candidate source of truth for the app refactor. A green
build is necessary but is not sufficient: every repository-owned file must be
reviewed, every finding must have evidence, and every claimed behavior must be
verified on the exact candidate commit.

## Visual Restoration Follow-Up

The author approved historical commit `bbbc315` as the visual reference on
2026-09-09. Current visual restoration coverage, findings, and screenshot
evidence are tracked in `docs/design/VISUAL_RESTORATION_TRACKER.md`. Existing
release constraints below remain in force. Prior functional verification does
not constitute approval of the refactored styling.

The restoration is implemented in `2f522c8` / `1b79465` and verified by the
`7798642` QA candidate: 143 UI/system/boundary files reviewed, 21 visual findings
verified, 94 reviewed iOS-engine captures, 883 local tests with coverage, passing
Linux tests and platform CI. Captures are test-fixture presentation evidence,
not live-payment, OS-keyboard, camera, or physical-device qualification. The
author's aesthetic review and the release blockers below remain separate.

The subsequent author review corrections are VIS-022 through VIS-025: remove
Home's provider credit, use plain recovery-copy icon/text, anchor import fields
to their description and size Market tiles to content. These are verified by
887 local tests with coverage, fatal analysis and a refreshed passing 94-image
iOS catalog. There are now 25 verified visual findings; no SDK or wallet
operation contract changed. Attribution approval remains part of FIAT-002.

The independent UI/UX audit on 2026-09-09 at `1fe2c1f` found **15
corrections** (3 P1, 12 P2), plus 3 design recommendations and explicit native
verification gaps. See the **Independent UI/UX Audit** section of
`docs/design/VISUAL_RESTORATION_TRACKER.md` for UXA-001 through UXA-015,
reproduction evidence and closure criteria. The earlier
VIS-001 through VIS-025 results remain historical evidence for their specific
changes, not whole-app accessibility or interaction certification. The follow-up
implementation closes all 15 code defects with host regression and visual
evidence. Its exact source identity and final command results are recorded in
the visual tracker's Implementation Closeout. The three optional recommendations
are excluded at the author's direction. Physical assistive traversal, native
walkthroughs and profiling are not inferred from host checks: ACCESS-019,
TEST-019 and PERF-015 remain blocked.

## Scope And Status

### October 13 SDK Upgrade (Active)

Starting app commit: `1abce21589cf7b14bf6c095a2878137fb8927407`.
SDK upgrade targets RN dev beta.37 at `e38329eba11cce16e3bea75b8a4e7becd52c0036`
and published RLN 0.16.0-beta.3 at `8777d71d7781b408fcce89c9c965ae29e8ad2300`.
Both were rechecked against GitHub on October 13. The app resolves public SDK
commit `7c44506fdfe1dae6f3a6fe884f849c7ca2ad028b`, not a local path override.
No wallet reset, palette/layout change, or mainnet mutation is part of this work.
Lightning remains a future release feature. WebRGB permissions do not by
themselves constitute a live WalletConnect pairing integration.

SDK-012 through SDK-017 are recorded in the authoritative Findings table below.

Verification caught 23 old policy/fixture failures, including SDK-017. Shared
expiry, duplicate-operation, amount, quarantine and navigation scenarios now
run against onchain routes; the existing native-adapter Lightning tests remain.
Direct Lightning app routes instead assert fail-closed behavior. No debug or
test-only switch was added to production code to bypass release policy. Lazy
list accessibility tests scroll to offscreen rows instead of assuming all rows
are built at 2x text. New checksums trust only the reviewed RLN 0.16 AAR/module/
POM and remove obsolete RLN 0.10/0.13 entries.

The fresh SDK consumer matrix was interrupted and invalidated by a changed
candidate plus disk exhaustion. Path-consumer Android/iOS builds and the Git
consumer Android build passed, but the complete exact-clean final matrix did
not. This does not qualify app runtime, clean consumers, funded BFA/Ethereum/
VSS, live WalletConnect, signed archives, physical devices or mainnet. All
simulators remained shut down; no foreign stack or wallet data was touched.

Statuses are `unreviewed`, `reviewed-clean`, `fixing`, `verified`, `blocked`,
and `accepted-constraint`. A finding is only `verified` when its implementation
and relevant tests/builds have passed on the recorded commit.

## SDK Upgrade Audit (2026-09-22)

The app previously resolved `3dd82e2` even though the SDK had advanced to
`734f5ae`. Historical verification below is evidence for the old candidate,
not for this upgrade. This audit covers every production SDK import and its
callers, fixtures, native packaging and integration documentation. No SDK
repository or wallet data is modified. UI styling is outside this change.

SDK-006 through SDK-010 below track this upgrade. The method-by-method contract
and intentionally unexposed SDK capabilities are documented in
`docs/architecture/SDK_INTEGRATION.md`. Existing SDK-002 through SDK-005 are
separate packaging findings and are not silently closed by updating the pin.

### Upgrade Verification

Evidence is for the working tree based on `47682a3`, not a committed or published
release. Diff SHA-256 excluding this tracker:
`177e18a4ee70584bf82ba407af5f1f667786693cab76f4a215bbdc34a752d60a`.
SDK repository remained untouched. No wallet was reset, no container or
simulator was booted/reused, and no payment was sent.

| Gate | Result | Evidence / scope |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Pub resolution | Pass | Public UTEXO immutable 734f5ae, no path override; only the SDK Pub dependency changed. |
| Regeneration and format | Pass | make verify-generated regenerates 447 inputs; final format check covers 470 Dart files with zero changes. |
| Fatal analyzer | Pass | flutter analyze --no-pub --fatal-warnings --fatal-infos, zero diagnostics. |
| Focused SDK regressions | Pass | 75 adapter/error tests, including invoice and channel limits, all RGB balance fields, aggregate overflow, requested versus actual allocations and new error types. |
| Full host suite | Pass | 965 tests; 14,771 / 16,689 covered lines (88.51%). LCOV SHA-256: 8ce36bda986e4887231187657049203d2453273f26a24e3946e8718abec828e7. |
| Repository gates | Pass | make verify: toolchain, architecture, design-system, barrel, governance, release config and tracker checks. No UI styling changes. |
| Dartdoc | Pass | 321 public libraries, zero warnings/errors. |
| iOS Simulator Debug | Pass | Flutter 3.47.0 / Dart 3.13.0, Xcode 27.0 (27A266a); CocoaPods lock regenerated. Native Swift and simulator archive SHA-256 match SDK baseline. |
| iOS XCTest target | Compile only | Isolated xcodebuild build-for-testing succeeds for generic iOS Simulator. No SDK Swift compiler warnings; dependency diagnostics retained as SDK-011. XCTest was not executed. |
| Android Debug | Pass | Initial strict checksum failure was fixed by regenerating metadata. Only RLN 0.13.0-beta.3 AAR/module entries retained; unrelated Kotlin 2.4.20 generation output discarded. Strict rebuild passes with Kotlin 2.4.0 runtime. APK SHA-256: 5f86ebf266211678c4c8f27750724e15a017b3ef8d2f88a1edfffb11db556aa5. |
| Android app-host tests | Pass | 3 tests, zero failures/errors/skips. These are app storage-channel tests, not the SDK bridge or funded smoke matrix. |
| Android native compatibility | Blocked | Debug APK ZIP alignment passes. Release verifier cannot qualify a Debug APK without libapp.so; independent readelf on checksum-verified AAR confirms both arm64/x86_64 RLN LOAD segments still use 0x1000. SDK-004 remains. |
| Runtime / release | Not qualified | No funded/unfunded app smokes, physical-device haptics/performance, signed release archives or mainnet qualification performed. SDK-010, existing release blockers and CON-001 remain. |

Detailed local command output is in `/tmp/aster-sdk-upgrade-*.log`; logs are
ephemeral and are not published as sanitized release evidence. New LSP/APay
and linked-asset product flows require a product/configuration decision; this
pass upgrades the existing application surface only.

## Accepted Constraints

| ID | Source | Status | Constraint | Release consequence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CON-001 | SDK PKG-006 | accepted-constraint | The pinned SDK accepts upstream native artifact provenance for internal beta and does not establish a funds-bearing mainnet production supply chain. | The app cannot truthfully claim funds-bearing mainnet production readiness while this remains accepted. |
| CON-002 | Product | accepted-constraint | No real swap backend is available. | Swap may provide selection and explanatory UI, but execution and success must remain unavailable. |

The provisional `com.example.asterdev` identity remains suitable only for development until release ownership supplies final identifiers and signing. |
| DEC-007 | verified | Use exact compatible releases `flutter_riverpod` 3.4.2, `riverpod_annotation` 4.0.6, `riverpod_generator` 4.0.8, and `build_runner` 2.16.0 on Flutter 3.47.0. | Flutter 3.47.0 advances the SDK-pinned analyzer/test graph enough to resolve the current Riverpod runtime and generator together. Generation, fatal analysis, and all host tests pass on Dart 3.13.0. |
| DEC-008 | verified | Keep `flutter_secure_storage` at 10.3.1 on this toolchain. | Version 11 still requires Android compile SDK 37 while Flutter 3.47.0 defines compile SDK 36. The pin was re-evaluated rather than inherited from the old toolchain; upgrade only with a coordinated platform contract. |
| DEC-009 | verified | Apply AGP built-in Kotlin to the Aster app module while retaining global legacy compatibility only for unmigrated dependency modules. | Flutter 3.47 and AGP 9.1 support module-by-module migration. Aster production and test Kotlin compile through `com.android.built-in-kotlin`; hosted plugins and immutable `rgb_sdk_flutter` still apply KGP, so the global opt-outs remain until PLAT-002 can close. |
| DEC-010 | verified | Keep iOS plugin integration on CocoaPods for the pinned SDK revision. | The SDK does not currently declare Swift Package Manager support. Flutter's generated package scaffolding remains tool-owned and ignored. |
| DEC-011 | verified | Store a random 256-bit native database password and the recovery mnemonic as one versioned per-wallet record in platform secure storage. | The product has no PIN/biometric unlock contract yet. iOS uses a non-synchronizing, this-device-only Keychain class; Android uses namespaced AES-GCM storage with destructive reset disabled. Platform round-trip tests pass at `1b3273a`; a later authentication UX may add user-presence controls without changing repository contracts. |
| DEC-012 | verified | Use one sealed `AppResult<T>` / `AppFailure` convention at repository and ViewModel boundaries. | SDK exceptions retain typed diagnostic causes internally, while UI receives stable redacted messages and retryability metadata. Fault and redaction tests pass at `1b3273a`. |
| DEC-013 | verified | Represent Bitcoin/RGB asset identity, payment rail, and blockchain environment as independent app-owned types. | Integer domain types and native boundary validation pass at `1b3273a`; the former display-string asset model and consumers were removed at `acd42ab`. |
| DEC-014 | verified | Serialize all SDK access through one app-owned wallet runtime owner. | Runtime, repository, lifecycle, switching, reinit, and fault-path tests pass at `1b3273a`. |
| DEC-015 | verified | Do not wrap native SDK calls in an app-side timeout that releases the serialization queue while native work may still be running. | The pinned SDK owns typed native timeout/quarantine behavior. The app adds bounded network timeouts around app-owned HTTP only; runtime ordering tests pass at `1b3273a`. |
| DEC-016 | verified | Protect wallet files with foreground-only iOS data protection and exclude them from backup; disable Android cloud backup and device transfer. | Native bridge and real platform tests prove the file policy; the exact Android release manifest proves backup/transfer exclusion at `1b3273a`. |
| DEC-017 | verified | Reject the pre-existing protected regtest host ports from app configuration. | Configuration tests at `1b3273a` reject `18443`, `50001`, and `3000` on loopback and Android emulator host aliases. No protected stack resource was inspected or mutated. |
| DEC-018 | verified | Preserve the established shape language: true stadium geometry for pills, rounded superellipses for cards/tiles/fields/sheets, and circles only for circular affordances. | ThemeData, shared controls, feature surfaces, shape tests, and the approved golden share this policy without changing palette or font values. |
| DEC-019 | verified | Runtime window insets are layout environment, not spacing tokens. | Typed `AsterWindowInsets` owns `padding`, `viewPadding`, and `viewInsets`; composition tests prove safe, system, and keyboard spaces remain distinct. |
| DEC-020 | verified | Route all tactile feedback through one injectable semantic haptic service. | Capability probing is shared/cached, failures cannot block actions, disabled/loading controls emit nothing, and direct plugin use is restricted to the adapter. |
| DEC-021 | verified | Present transient messages through one bounded FIFO toast queue with deduplication and an app-root host. | The bounded injectable controller supports success/failure/warning/info, accessible dismissal, safe text, deterministic advancement, and reduced motion. |
| DEC-022 | verified | Resolve animation durations through one reduced-motion policy and mute offscreen feature tickers. | Decorative loops settle when motion is disabled; navigation/toast/component transitions use effective durations; inactive tabs disable tickers and interaction. |
| DEC-023 | verified | Use the canonical Bitcoin BIPs English BIP39 list for per-word UX, while the pinned SDK remains authoritative for full mnemonic checksum validation. | The exact 2,048-word UTF-8 source is vendored with immutable provenance and SHA-256; byte, shape, order, uniqueness, membership, corruption, and concurrent-load tests pass at `5f3698f` and `05c7edf`. |
| DEC-024 | verified | Persist app preferences through one typed, versioned settings repository and generated Riverpod ViewModel. | Currency, region, Bitcoin unit, and balance visibility survive repository reconstruction; defaults preserve current behavior (`USD`, `US`, `BTC`), writes are serialized, and Home consumes durable visibility/unit state at `acd42ab`. |
| DEC-025 | verified | Keep recovery phrases route-scoped and minimize copies rather than pretending Dart strings can be zeroized. | No phrase enters logs, analytics, route arguments, durable preferences, or global wallet state. Route-scoped controllers/state release references on disposal; a serialized digest-only clipboard lease clears only matching content after a bounded interval or screen exit, with fault/disposal tests passing at `05c7edf`. Screenshot policy remains the separate SEC-011 decision. |
| DEC-026 | verified | Route access is derived from the durable active-wallet state. | Empty registries enter onboarding; wallet-only routes redirect to onboarding; confirmed provisioning reloads registry state before replacing navigation with Home. Pure-policy and real GoRouter tests pass at `518b077`; configured native restart evidence remains ROUTE-002 rather than weakening this invariant. |
| DEC-027 | blocked | Do not expose seed or xpub disclosure actions before SEC-007 is decided. | Advanced settings may expose truthful network diagnostics now, but sensitive rows remain disabled and explicitly unavailable rather than bypassing authentication policy. |
| DEC-028 | verified | Accept bounded multiword paste at the import fields without programmatically reading the system clipboard. | Input is lowercased, stripped to canonical word characters, capped to 12 BIP39-sized words, distributed from the focused field, and still requires SDK checksum validation; focused UX and adversarial tests pass at `05c7edf`. |
| DEC-029 | verified | Support exactly one current Aster wallet-storage schema before the first public release. | Pre-release development data has no compatibility promise. Obsolete development installs must be cleared or restored from their recovery phrase; production code contains no compatibility reader, data converter, or old-key fallback. Crash-safe reconciliation for interrupted writes in the current schema remains required. |
| DEC-030 | verified | Stable feature navigation carries a product asset/rail target; repositories resolve it to deployment-owned contract metadata before wallet execution. | Prevents route arguments, ticker text, or presentation models from substituting an RGB contract ID or precision. Focused domain/configuration/repository tests pass in the Phase 5 foundation slice. |
| DEC-031 | verified | Own Bitcoin payment-URI policy in the app, using exact-pinned pure-Dart codecs only for Base58Check and Bech32/Bech32m primitives. | User-entered and native-returned on-chain addresses pass checksum, witness-program, and configured-network validation at `021a610`. BIP321 amounts use integer satoshis, unknown optional instructions are accepted only with a valid selected-rail destination, and unsupported `req-*` instructions fail closed. Route-aware embedded Lightning handling is verified by DEC-032/PAY-003 at `633537e`. |
| DEC-032 | verified | A product route selects Bitcoin on-chain or Lightning before a BIP321 URI is decoded; URI contents never switch rails. | The selected on-chain route accepts a valid fallback or the first compatible repeated `bc`/`tb`/`bcrt` instruction in sender order. The selected Lightning route extracts exactly one BOLT11 instruction and rejects multiple invoices because the app has no invoice-choice UI. Unsupported `req-*` instructions fail closed; optional `pop` is never opened or retained. Exact aggregate verification passes at `633537e`. |
| DEC-033 | verified | On-chain fee quotes are display-only outside the repository; every send refreshes its own native estimate inside the serialized mutation transaction. | Prevents presentation code or a stale screen quote from supplying the actual mutation fee. Fractional native estimates round up to integer sats/vbyte, malformed output fails closed, and Lightning commands structurally reject irrelevant on-chain fee data. Exact aggregate verification passes at `1d0627a`. |
| DEC-034 | verified | Send and Receive state is route-scoped by the stable product target; destination, invoice, amount, fee, and generated-request values never enter URLs or GoRouter extras. | Generated auto-dispose family ViewModels preserve the selected asset/rail while keeping payment material in memory only. Stable target routes, invalid-target redirects, direct amount-link rejection, and screen/router behavior pass at `715e671` and `db844ac`. |
| DEC-035 | verified | Time-sensitive app behavior uses one injectable UTC clock and schedules UI invalidation at the owning domain deadline. | Fee freshness, Lightning expiry, market-quote freshness, HTTP-date retry policy, Send/Receive countdowns, and mounted Home quote invalidation are deterministic at `814ec5c` and `acd42ab`; wall-clock reads do not hide inside feature calculations. |
| DEC-036 | verified | Normalize Bitcoin transactions, RGB transfers, and Lightning payments behind the wallet adapter into one app-owned activity contract. | IDs are opaque deterministic hashes of source-native identity, duplicate IDs must carry identical data, money remains exact integer units, missing native timestamps/amounts/directions remain explicitly absent, and only the resulting typed model reaches Activity presentation or routes. Adapter/domain/repository/ViewModel/widget/router tests and both platform builds pass at `e9d7fab`. |
| DEC-037 | verified | Transaction explorer actions use a validated deployment-owned base URL and one injectable platform boundary. | Only canonical 64-hex Bitcoin transaction IDs are opened, only public HTTPS bases without credentials/query/fragment are accepted, and regtest has no default explorer. Flutter-team `url_launcher` 6.3.2 plus resolved iOS 6.4.1/Android 6.3.32 carry hosted SHA-256 identities and BSD-3-Clause licenses; fault tests and both builds pass at `e9d7fab`. |
| DEC-038 | verified | Own each Scan camera/gallery session behind one injectable app contract, with distinct persistent-tab and destination-picker scopes. | Isolated plugin adapters, one keep-alive factory command queue across all native sessions, serialized lifecycle/cleanup, bounded redacted payloads, applied native state, Android lost-data recovery, and explicit picker/launcher completion contracts pass aggregate and platform-build gates at `b606ebf`. Physical-device behavior remains honestly isolated under TEST-001/ACCESS-001. |
| DEC-039 | verified | Treat package metadata and public support/legal/social destinations as separate app-owned Settings dependencies. | Commit `89c8d7f` obtains bounded runtime metadata through an injectable `package_info_plus` adapter, validates optional public HTTPS destinations fail-closed, keeps absent links visibly disabled, maps launch faults to typed recoverable failures, and proves metadata/link independence under overlap and malformed configuration tests. |
| DEC-040 | verified | Aster owns the product, app, UI, platform resources, storage namespaces, environment variables, logs, and tooling; UTEXO names only the RGB protocol integration boundary. | `UtexoWallet`, the `utexo` SDK signet carrier, UTEXO service endpoints, Maven coordinates, and the public SDK repository remain because they are external protocol/API identities. They must not be repurposed as Aster product branding. Final platform IDs remain DEC-006. |
| DEC-041 | verified | A confirmed foreground resume reopens the native wallet first, then invalidates read-only Home and Activity projections without forcing a network sync or discarding route-scoped mutation forms. | Native reopen is a security/correctness prerequisite. Home intentionally exposes no pull-to-refresh gesture; provider invalidation, lifecycle recovery, and the market owner's bounded background retry remain the truthful read paths. Exact `3fc63ba` host, Android background/foreground, and platform build evidence passes; HOME-007 owns the later gesture removal. |
| DEC-042 | verified | Quote Bitcoin and Tether directly in the selected CoinGecko-supported fiat instead of composing a second-provider FX rate. Carry ISO code, symbol, and minor-unit precision through the typed market contract; retain only exact integer monetary math. | CoinGecko's official Simple Price and Supported Currencies contracts explicitly support multi-currency portfolio reporting. A bundled reviewed fiat intersection prevents dead settings choices; live payload validation and truthful unavailable states pass the exact `d449b5d` host/platform gate. |
| DEC-043 | verified | Keep CoinGecko's keyless public pool development-only. Release builds require a deployment-owned HTTPS market proxy and never embed a third-party provider key. | CoinGecko documents the keyless pool as testing/prototyping infrastructure with dynamic shared limits. Release composition fails closed before transport when unconfigured. The author subsequently removed Home's provider credit under VIS-022; approved attribution placement or a contractual exception remains part of blocked FIAT-002, not an assumed waiver. |
| DEC-044 | verified | Disable Riverpod's container-level automatic provider-build retry. | Explicit ViewModel commands own user-visible retry, and `ResilientHttpClient` alone owns bounded idempotent GET retry. The production composition root rejects an implicit policy, and an exact delayed regression at `ce03a8f` proves a typed provider failure remains at one build beyond Riverpod's first default 200 ms retry boundary. |
| DEC-045 | verified | Aster's app-owned blockchain environment accepts only `mainnet`, `signet`, and `regtest`; the pinned SDK's `bitcoin` and `utexo` carriers are translated only inside `RgbWalletEngine`. | Asset identity, blockchain environment, and protocol wire vocabulary remain separate. Direct parser and SDK-adapter vectors prevent a future compatibility alias from leaking the UTEXO protocol name into product configuration or durable app models. |
| DEC-046 | verified | Keep `flutter_secure_storage` 10.3.1 algorithm bootstrap enabled only inside the isolated Aster namespace, with its backup compatibility path and destructive reset disabled. | The pinned Android implementation treats absent cipher markers as historical PKCS1/CBC defaults even on first use; disabling its algorithm-change path therefore rejects a clean OAEP/GCM namespace. This plugin-internal marker bootstrap cannot read another namespace and is distinct from an app wallet-schema migration. Source policy locks the required combination. |
| DEC-047 | verified | Keep market recovery silent: retain validated content, skeletonize missing price/history, and show only a two-line tappable offline chart state for classified connectivity failures. | Market reads are idempotent and recover through cancellable exponential backoff with jitter and `Retry-After` ownership. Provider, protocol, configuration, rate-limit, and server copy never leaks into the Home artwork; explicit offline activation resets backoff and retries immediately. |
| DEC-048 | verified | Confirm generated recovery phrases at fixed words 3, 6, 9, and 12 before wallet creation. | The author required stable positions rather than a random sequence. Four checks provide one prompt in each three-word block, consistent with the one-in-three confirmation model documented by Blockstream Jade. Production uses cryptographically strong randomness for distinct BIP39 distractors and option order, then stores each question once so rebuilds cannot reshuffle it; tests inject seeded randomness. |
| DEC-049 | verified | Require a user-owned wallet name before create or import provisioning and use the same create flow from Wallet Selection. | No hardcoded `Primary Wallet` or `Restored Wallet` production name remains. Naming is part of the route-scoped provisioning state, validated before native work, and committed atomically with the wallet registry. |
| DEC-050 | verified | Preserve Flutter's platform page-transition motion and iOS interactive back gesture while suppressing only the Cupertino edge-shadow decoration. | Android keeps predictive back and iOS keeps the standard curve, parallax, directionality, and gesture detector. Replacing the private gesture owner or inventing cross-platform motion would be a behavioral regression. |
| DEC-051 | verified | Home exposes no user-driven pull-to-refresh gesture. | Wallet projections refresh through lifecycle/provider ownership; market data silently retries in its idempotent owner. Removing the gesture avoids an unrequested global refresh affordance without fabricating a wallet sync. |

## Current Working-Tree Verification (2026-09-17)

This evidence covers HOME-007, ONB-011, ONB-012, WALLET-004, NAV-007, and
MARKET-014 on the current uncommitted working tree. No commit or push was
requested for this slice.

| Check | Result | Exact evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Generation | Pass | `fvm dart run build_runner build` processed 446 inputs and wrote the new Riverpod provider output; the final repository verification rerun reported 444 skipped inputs, 2 no-op inputs, and zero outputs. |
| Format and analysis | Pass | `make format-check` checked 472 Dart files with zero changes. `fvm flutter analyze --no-pub --fatal-warnings --fatal-infos` completed with zero diagnostics. |
| API documentation | Pass | `make docs` regenerated zero source outputs and completed warning-free, error-free Dartdoc for all 320 public libraries. |
| Focused behavior | Pass | 94 Home, market lifecycle, onboarding, wallet-selection, and real-router tests pass, followed by a dedicated iOS edge-swipe regression. Coverage includes first-run create, Add Wallet, fixed-position confirmation, canonical secure-choice invariants, naming, current-screen loading, native-commit navigation, and shadowless interactive pop. |
| Complete host suite | Pass after bounded rerun | The default high-concurrency run passed 934 tests and timed out one process-fixture test at its hard 10-second deadline. | SDK-007 | P1 | Exact RGB values | verified | RGB balances, assignments, channel and invoice/payment amounts changed from int to BigInt. Unchecked narrowing would corrupt money above signed range. | All production SDK import boundaries | working tree | Checked conversions and overflow regressions pass across balances, channels, invoice decode, payments and assignment sums; app signed-64 domain limitation is explicit. |
| SDK-008 | P1 | Transfer history | verified | WaitingBroadcast was unknown; unallocated pending receives failed the feed. Requested amounts must not masquerade as allocated funds. | RGB activity adapter versus CoreTransfer | working tree | Dedicated status plus pending/failed amount-unknown behavior, settled allocation precedence, non-fungible and overflow vectors pass in full host suite and both platform builds. |
| SDK-009 | P2 | Defaults and errors | verified | New LNURL business failures and LSP quote/proof rejection types inherited retryable connectivity classification. LSP default ownership also needed review. | Configuration resolver and failure mapper | working tree | Redacted non-retryable semantic failures and mainnet/signet/default/override tests pass. Stable SDK does not export its LSP resolver, so explicit reviewed app policy remains; no private import added. |
| SDK-010 | P1 | Candidate runtime evidence | blocked | Exact new-candidate native funded/unfunded flows and restart/switch behavior have not been executed. SDK LSP/APay/linked/VSS success-path evidence is also incomplete; those APIs are not automatically app features. | SDK release tracker and app integration audit | - | Compile-only evidence is not runtime qualification. Obtain isolated app-owned regtest and both-platform funded/unfunded lifecycle/payment evidence, plus product/infrastructure contracts before exposing new SDK feature families. CON-001 still forbids a funds-bearing mainnet production claim. |
| SDK-011 | P2 | Non-SDK compiler diagnostics | blocked | A fresh Xcode 27 test-target build reports deprecated image_picker/share_plus APIs, mobile_scanner unused/deprecated API and generated-header ownership warnings. AppIntents metadata extraction also reports no framework dependency. | Isolated generic-Simulator build-for-testing log on 2026-09-22 | - | Build succeeds and no rgb_sdk_flutter Swift compiler warning remains. ## White Onboarding And No Tap Ink (2026-09-10)

- Implementation and verification source: `9f7c014404ec589b4f8cf268103016d1b6cb74b7`.
  This follow-up only records that identity; executable inputs are unchanged.
- Welcome, Create and Import remain separate GoRouter pages. Only Welcome
  exposes the retained mesh; the white recovery pages preserve content and
  use the existing dark-text/neutral-surface/primary-blue roles. No palette,
  font, wallet, SDK, network, or native configuration was changed.
- The initial aggregate passed 918 tests and exported 94 host fixture images
  under `build/white-onboarding-2026-09-10`. Welcome, Create, Import, loading,
  failure and keyboard-inset layouts were visually reviewed. These are public
  test-phrase fixtures, not captures of the user's live wallet.
- Independent test review corrected the ink capture boundary and added a
  default-theme detection control. All five strengthened no-splash tests pass;
  the final aggregate passes all 919 tests after that test-only correction.
- A later Welcome-only wordmark experiment was rejected by the author. The
  existing Aster header remains on Welcome, Create, and Import; the white
  recovery surfaces and every other onboarding layout and behavior remain
  unchanged. Restoration source:
  `2e75a4972c3f09b207afd3e77fa5851559114771`.
- `make lint` passes format, generation consistency, fatal analysis, Dartdoc
  (316 public libraries), architecture, 122 UI sources, 79 barrels, governance,
  configuration and tracker checks. The iOS Simulator debug build succeeds
  using the ignored Aster-only regtest configuration. The existing SDK-002
  Swift Package Manager warning remains, with CocoaPods integration successful.
- After the author-requested wallet reset, the dedicated Aster simulator was
  erased, reinstalled, and relaunched on Welcome with no stale wallet or
  Keychain state. No live recovery phrase was captured. Android-themed host
  rendering is tested, but no native Android build/run or new physical-device
  interaction qualification is claimed for this styling change. Existing
  ACCESS-019, TEST-019, PERF-015 and all other release blockers remain open.
- Inventory totals are 580 files (516 verified, 46 reviewed-clean, 18 blocked);
  finding totals are 435 (404 verified, 31 blocked). The two accepted constraints
  are separate.

## Local Regtest Session (2026-09-10)

- No Aster Compose project existed before this session. `aster-app-regtest`
  now owns new containers, network, bind-mounted state, independent RPC
  credentials, and ports 19443/51001/3100. Existing Iris/SDK/protected stacks
  and their persistent state were not modified.
- All four services report healthy; real Bitcoin RPC reports regtest with
  initial block download complete, Electrum headers advance, and proxy
  `server.info` returns version 0.3.0 / protocol 0.2. The faucet starts with
  mature test coins and mines every ten seconds. No app wallet was funded.
- Native port 9736 was occupied by Iris. CONFIG-013 gives Aster its own
  19735/19736 configuration before wallet creation; no other process stopped.
- Build-cache cleanup removed 78 audited inactive compiler/cache targets
  (56.79 GiB allocated) plus 9.695 GB of Docker build cache. Free disk rose
  from approximately 10 GiB to 73 GiB before rebuilding. Source, dependency
  downloads needed for offline rebuild, reports/screenshots, wallet data,
  simulators, Docker images/volumes, and active Iris build trees were retained.
  An idle Gradle daemon was stopped; no active compiler was interrupted.
- New stack image packaging provenance is development-only, not a production
  supply-chain approval. LSP, Lightning liquidity, USDT contract, VSS, funded
  rail tests, and physical-device tests are not claimed by this setup.
- Verification: 913 host tests pass; fatal analysis is clean; Dartdoc validates
  316 public libraries without warnings/errors; architecture, design, barrels,
  release-configuration, governance, and tracker checks pass. The first lint
  attempt rejected an external stack name in the new guide as retired product
  vocabulary; the wording was corrected and all policy gates rerun.
- The iOS Debug build completed in 21.1 seconds and was installed/launched on
  Aster-UI-Baseline-QA / iOS 26.5. All five generated Xcode Dart defines match
  the ignored local JSON exactly. The user began manual wallet creation during
  the final UI check; the incidental temporary recovery-screen capture was
  deleted and the user warned to treat that QA phrase as exposed/regtest-only.
  No wallet secret is stored in this repository or report. Simulator controls
  were left to the user; no funded send/receive result is claimed.
  Android was not rebuilt or run in this session. SDK SwiftPM warning remains
  SDK-002. Source-equivalent evidence belongs to the commit adding this section.

## Closeout Requirements

- No `unreviewed` or `fixing` file rows.
- No open finding except a documented `accepted-constraint` or a decision that
  truly requires external ownership.
- Clean second code-generation run, fatal analysis, tests, and warning-free
  `dart doc` on the exact candidate commit.
- iOS and Android build/run evidence on the exact candidate commit.
- Profile-mode frame, launch, memory, and leak evidence with device/tool details.
- Release report keeps CON-001 visible and does not claim funds-bearing mainnet
  readiness while the SDK supply-chain constraint remains accepted.
